======================================================================
HEALTH KNOWLEDGE BASE
======================================================================

Entry 1
Category: Human Body
Concept: Health
Definition: A state of complete physical, mental and social well-being.
Key Points:
- Fundamental concept in health and medicine.
- Important for maintaining personal and public health.
- Frequently covered in biology, health science and competitive examinations.
Example:
Health is an important health concept.
Related Concepts:
Human Body, Nutrition, Disease Prevention, Medicine, Fitness, Public Health

Entry 12
Category: Nutrition & Fitness
Concept: Balanced Diet
